South Carolina (SC) SLED
Transport: TCP/IP DMPP2020 with AES Encryption extensions

Disclaimer Statement

Routing and Configuration Information: Mnemonic Routing
 The SLED assigned ORI for a device (or for the site) should be placed in the <Authentication>/<ORI> field in the ConnectCIC header.
 The SLED assigned Mnemonic for a device should be placed in the <Authentication>/<Mnemonic> field in the ConnectCIC header.

Security Requirements:
Each agency must complete the necessary requirements and paperwork to meet the SLED CJIS NCIC security requirements prior to SLED allowing the agency access to CJIS. Information regarding these requirements can be found on the SLED Website: http://www.sled.sc.gov/CurrentRequirements.aspx?MenuID=CJIS. See the document "SLED NCIC System Usage Requirements." Appendix B provides vendor details. However, to ensure you have the latest requirements, we recommend having your sponsor agency request the current documentation and requirements from SLED.

Logon Requirements: None

Certification Requirements:
Each agency must submit a request letter to SLED outlining the solution, vendors involved, etc. Each agency must also complete the SLED CJIS Site Security Survey. Agency and Vendor requirements are listed in the document SLED NCIC System Usage Requirements, Appendix B. Information regarding these requirements can be found on the SLED Website: http://www.sled.sc.gov/CurrentRequirements.aspx?MenuID=CJIS. See the document "SLED NCIC System Usage Requirements." Appendix B provides vendor details. However, to ensure you have the latest requirements, we recommend having your sponsor agency request the current documentation and requirements from SLED.
SLED only recognizes the vendor who "owns" the agency; therefore, SLED will not individually certify the CommSys ConnectCIC, as it is considered just part of the vendor's solution. We have our vendor documents and fingerprints on file at SLED so

they do know about us, but it's the "front-end" vendor that receives certification from SLED.
